# THE PUNICODEX BRAND-TEMPLE ATLAS
## A Comprehensive Mapping of Modern Commerce to Ancient Archetypes
**Version 1.0 | 2026-11-11**

---

# INTRODUCTION

This atlas maps real-world companies, brands, and institutions to their most resonant PuniCodex flagship temples. Each connection is based on shared archetypal DNA: a company's core function, brand mythology, market positioning, or historical narrative aligns with a deity whose domain has governed that same force for millennia.

A single brand may claim multiple temples. Nike (the company) resonates with Nike (victory) but also with Hermes (speed), Athena (strategy), and Nemesis (the cost of dominance). The mapping is not one-to-one. It is **many-to-many** — a web of resonance rather than a ledger of ownership.

The format for each entry:

**Brand → [Temple](/temple/)** — Bespoke explanation of the archetypal connection.

---

# SECTOR 1: SPORTS, FITNESS & ATHLETICS

**Nike → [Nike](/nike/)** — The most obvious mapping in commercial history. Nike (the company) named itself after Nike (the goddess) and built a $50 billion empire on the same principle: victory as identity. The goddess crowned warriors on battlefields; the company crowns athletes on podiums. Both understand that victory is not an outcome. It is a **language** — the swoosh and the winged sandal speak the same dialect of aspiration.

**Nike → [Hermes](/hermes/)** — Beyond victory, Nike the company is a logistics and speed machine. The Air technology, the sub-two-hour marathon attempt, the "Just Do It" urgency — this is Hermes energy. The winged god of swift passage and competitive commerce. Every same-day delivery, every sub-4-minute mile, every rush-to-market product drop is a Hermetic transaction.

**Nike → [Nemesis](/nemesis/)** — The darker twin. Nike dominates 27% of the global athletic footwear market. That dominance has attracted antitrust scrutiny, labour criticism, and cultural backlash. Nemesis is the goddess who punishes hubris. Every sweatshop exposé, every athlete scandal, every cancelled contract is Nemesis answering. The brand that claims Nike without acknowledging Nemesis is incomplete.

**Adidas → [Nike](/nike/)** — Founded by a German shoemaker who named his company after himself (Adi Dassler), Adidas has spent 75 years chasing the same victory archetype as Nike. The three stripes are a crown by another name. The rivalry is the Judgement of Paris with rubber soles.

**Adidas → [Ares](/ares/)** — Adidas's brand history is more combative than Nike's. The 1970s battles with Puma (founded by Dassler's brother), the aggressive sponsorship of violent sports (boxing, MMA, rugby), the military-adjacent aesthetic of Originals. Ares is the patron of combat sports — not the noble contest but the raw clash.

**Puma → [Ares](/ares/)** — Founded by Rudolf Dassler in a feud with his brother Adolf (Adidas), Puma's entire origin story is fratricidal war. The leaping cat is a predator, not a racer. Ares governs both the violence of sport and the violence of sibling rivalry.

**Under Armour → [Ares](/ares/)** — "Protect this house." The brand was built on compression gear for American football — the most violent mainstream sport. Its entire aesthetic is armour. The name itself references the protective layer beneath the uniform. Ares would wear Under Armour.

**Under Armour → [Herakles](/herakles/)** — The brand's origin story: Kevin Plank, a former Maryland football player, created the first compression shirt because he was tired of sweating through cotton. A personal labour that became a commercial empire. Herakles performed Twelve Labours; Plank performed one that spawned a hundred product lines.

**Lululemon → [Artemis](/artemis/)** — Yoga pants for trail runners. The brand's entire mythology is about the wilderness — not the gym, not the stadium, but the mountain trail at dawn. Artemis is the goddess of the hunt and the wilderness athlete. Lululemon's community events are not competitions. They are **expeditions**.

**Lululemon → [Aphrodite](/aphrodite/)** — The secondary resonance is undeniable. Lululemon built a $15 billion company by making athletic wear aspirational — the "athleisure" category is Aphrodite's domain. The body as aesthetic object, the practice as self-presentation, the community as courtship ritual.

**Athleta → [Artemis](/artemis/)** — Gap's athletic brand for women, explicitly positioned as the outdoor alternative to gym-centric competitors. The name itself derives from the Greek "athlete." Artemis is the explicit patron.

**Fabletics → [Aphrodite](/aphrodite/)** — Kate Hudson's subscription activewear brand is built on celebrity association and body-conscious design. The "fable" in the name hints at mythology, but the real resonance is with desire and aspiration — Aphrodite's core domain.

**Gymshark → [Herakles](/herakles/)** — Founded by a 19-year-old British bodybuilder who screenprinted shirts in his parents' garage. The brand built its empire through YouTube fitness influencers performing extreme physical labours. Herakles would subscribe to Gymshark's channel.

**Rogue Fitness → [Herakles](/herakles/)** — The equipment supplier of CrossFit and strongman competitions. Rogue's entire brand is about the extreme end of physical capacity — thousand-pound deadlifts, atlas stones, yoke carries. These are Labours in modern form.

**Rogue Fitness → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — Rogue manufactures its own equipment in Columbus, Ohio. The forged barbell, the welded rack, the machined collar — this is Hephaistos's forge in industrial form. The company that makes the tools for Herakles must be Hephaistos.

**CrossFit → [Herakles](/herakles/)** — The WOD (Workout of the Day) is a Labour. The Games are the Olympiad. The culture of suffering-as-transformation is the core Herakles narrative: pain produces excellence. Greg Glassman created a religion of self-inflicted hardship.

**CrossFit → [Nemesis](/nemesis/)** — The injury rate, the rhabdomyolysis cases, the founder's controversial departure, the community fractures. Nemesis answers every cult of excess. CrossFit's decline from cultural dominance is a Nemesis event.

**Equinox → [Apollo](/apollo/)** — The luxury gym chain that costs $200+/month. Not a place to sweat. A place to be **seen** sweating. Apollo governs both physical perfection and social display. The Equinox member is not exercising. They are performing virtue.

**Equinox → [Aphrodite](/aphrodite/)** — The secondary resonance: Equinox is a dating venue disguised as a gym. The lighting, the clientele, the cocktail bar attached to every location. Aphrodite would hold her membership there.

**SoulCycle → [Dionysos](/dionysos/)** — The dark room, the loud music, the ecstatic group experience, the instructor-as-shaman. SoulCycle is not exercise. It is **ritual**. Dionysos governed exactly this: collective altered states achieved through physical exertion and sensory overload.

**Peloton → [Nike](/nike/)** — The name references the lead pack in a bicycle race — the front group that dictates pace. Victory through positioning. But Peloton's real mythology is about **domestic conquest** — winning the battle against laziness from your living room.

**Peloton → [Kronos](/kronos/)** — The connected fitness data, the leaderboard, the streaks, the scheduled classes. Peloton is a time-management system disguised as a bike. Kronos governs the quantified self.

**Strava → [Nike](/nike/)** — The social network for athletes. Every segment is a mini-Olympiad. The KOM (King of the Mountain) crown is Nike's laurel in digital form. Strava does not measure fitness. It measures **victory**.

**Strava → [Nemesis](/nemesis/)** — The "Strava or it didn't happen" culture, the KOM hunting that leads to reckless riding, the deaths of cyclists chasing segments downhill. Nemesis answers every competitive impulse that ignores safety.

**Garmin → [Kronos](/kronos/)** — Originally a GPS navigation company, Garmin pivoted to wearable fitness trackers that measure every temporal dimension of athletic performance: pace per mile, heart rate over time, recovery duration. Kronos quantified.

**Garmin → [Helios](/helios/)** — The solar-powered outdoor watches (Instinct Solar, Fenix Solar) that harvest energy from the sun. Helios's chariot powers the device that measures the ride.

**Fitbit → [Kronos](/kronos/)** — The original quantified-self wearable. Steps, sleep, heart rate, calories — every biometric reduced to a time-series graph. Fitbit made Kronos personal.

**Fitbit → [Hypnos](/hypnos/)** — The sleep tracking feature was Fitbit's most differentiated capability. Hypnos governs the night; Fitbit measures his domain.

**Oura → [Hypnos](/hypnos/)** — The Oura Ring is primarily a sleep tracker. Its entire value proposition is nocturnal recovery — HRV, body temperature, sleep stages. Hypnos is the explicit patron.

**Whoop → [Hypnos](/hypnos/)** — Strain and recovery. The dual metric of exertion and rest. Whoop's entire philosophy is that performance is determined by what happens during sleep. Hypnos and Nike in continuous negotiation.

**Therabody → [Asklepios](/asklepios/)** — The massage gun company (Theragun) that pivoted to a full recovery ecosystem. Percussive therapy, pneumatic compression, electrical stimulation — these are Asklepios's tools in consumer form.

**Hyperice → [Asklepios](/asklepios/)** — The other massage gun giant. Recovery as medicine. The brand's entire positioning is therapeutic: reduce inflammation, accelerate healing, prevent injury. Asklepios would prescribe the Hypervolt.

**Red Bull → [Nike](/nike/)** — "Gives you wings." The slogan is Nike's iconography in beverage form. Red Bull owns extreme sports — Formula 1, cliff diving, space jumping. Victory through danger.

**Red Bull → [Helios](/helios/)** — The energy itself. The caffeine-and-taurine rush is solar power in liquid form. Helios's chariot in a can.

**Red Bull → [Prometheus](/prometheus/)** — The brand's mythology is about pushing human limits — Felix Baumgartner's space jump, the Flugtag, the Crashed Ice. Prometheus stole fire to elevate humanity. Red Bull sells the fire.

**Monster Energy → [Typhon](/typhon/)** — The claw-mark logo, the extreme sports sponsorship, the "Unleash the Beast" tagline. Typhon was the most monstrous creature in Greek mythology — a hundred dragon heads, fire-breathing, storm-raising. Monster Energy is Typhon in beverage form.

**Gatorade → [Nike](/nike/)** — Invented at the University of Florida to help football players win. The entire brand is about athletic victory through hydration. The Gatorade bath is the modern laurel crown.

**Gatorade → [Asklepios](/asklepios/)** — The sports science angle: electrolyte replacement, carbohydrate delivery, cramp prevention. Gatorade is medicine for athletes. Asklepios's sports drink.

**Powerade → [Nike](/nike/)** — Coca-Cola's answer to Gatorade. Same archetype, different parent company. The rivalry is Hermes (Coca-Cola) vs. Nike (Gatorade/Pepsi) in the beverage aisle.

**BodyArmor → [Asklepios](/asklepios/)** — Positioned as the "healthier" sports drink — coconut water base, natural flavours, no artificial colours. The armour metaphor is Hephaistos; the health positioning is Asklepios.

**Prime Hydration → [Nike](/nike/)** — Founded by YouTube boxing rivals Logan Paul and KSI, Prime is hydration as victory merchandise. The brand's entire mythology is competitive — two fighters, one drink.

**Celsius → [Helios](/helios/)** — The name references temperature. The brand promises to "burn calories" — metabolic heat. Helios is the sun that burns.

**Bang Energy → [Prometheus](/prometheus/)** — "Fuel your destiny." The extreme caffeine content, the bodybuilding culture, the promise of superhuman performance. Prometheus's fire in beverage form.

**5-hour Energy → [Kronos](/kronos/)** — The name is explicitly temporal. Five hours of compressed productivity. Kronos in a shot bottle.

**C4 Energy → [Ares](/ares/)** — The explosive pre-workout brand. C4 is a plastic explosive. The brand mythology is about detonation — the moment of maximum force. Ares governs the explosion.

**Optimum Nutrition → [Asklepios](/asklepios/)** — "The Gold Standard." The brand's positioning is about purity, potency, and clinical validation. Nutrition as medicine.

**MyProtein → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The European supplement giant that democratised protein powder through aggressive pricing and scale. Ploutos governs abundance through commerce.

**MuscleTech → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The supplement company that built its brand on "engineered" products — Nitro-Tech, Cell-Tech, Hydroxycut. The "Tech" in the name is Hephaistos's forge.

**Transparent Labs → [Thoth](/thoth/)** — The supplement brand that built its entire identity on ingredient transparency and third-party testing. Thoth governs truth in formulation.

**Onnit → [Asklepios](/asklepios/)** — Founded by Joe Rogan, Onnit built its brand on nootropics and human optimisation. Total Human Optimization is Asklepios's manifesto.

**Alpha Brain → [Athena](/athena/)** — The nootropic that promises cognitive enhancement. Athena is the goddess of wisdom and strategic thinking.

**Ashwagandha supplements → [Shiva](/shiva/)** — The "smell of horse" herb in Ayurveda, used for strength and stress reduction. Shiva's adaptogen.

**Rhodiola Rosea supplements → [Sif](/sif/)** — The golden root used by Vikings for endurance. Sif is the Norse goddess associated with golden hair and endurance.

**Shilajit supplements → [Dhanvantari](/dhanvantari/)** — The Himalayan mineral pitch used in Ayurveda for vitality and longevity. Dhanvantari's mountain medicine.

**WWE → [Ares](/ares/)** — Professional wrestling is scripted combat, but the archetype is genuine. Ares governs the spectacle of violence, the ritualised conflict that entertains without killing.

**WWE → [Dionysos](/dionysos/)** — The theatricality, the personas, the crowd participation, the soap opera narratives. WWE is Dionysian theatre with body slams.

**UFC → [Ares](/ares/)** — The ultimate expression of unarmed combat. The Octagon is Ares's temple. Every knockout is a sacrifice to the war god.

**UFC → [Nike](/nike/)** — The championship belt, the ranking system, the pursuit of undisputed titles. Victory in its most visceral form.

**ONE Championship → [Buddha](/buddha/)** — The Asian martial arts promotion that emphasises respect, humility, and spiritual growth alongside combat. Buddha governs the martial artist who fights without anger.

**Bellator → [Ares](/ares/)** — The Latin word for "warrior." The name is the claim. Ares is the explicit patron.

**PFL → [Nike](/nike/)** — The Professional Fighters League with its season format and championship prize. Victory through format innovation.

**Top Rank → [Nike](/nike/)** — Bob Arum's boxing promotion empire. Six decades of crowning champions. Nike's laurel in the squared circle.

**Matchroom Boxing → [Nike](/nike/)** — Eddie Hearn's global boxing machine. The silver briefcase, the press conferences, the build — all in service of the knockout victory.

**Golden Boy Promotions → [Nike](/nike/)** — Oscar De La Hoya's company. The "Golden Boy" himself was a Nike athlete. The resonance is literal.

**Mayweather Promotions → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — Floyd Mayweather's company. His nickname is "Money." His entire brand is wealth accumulation through combat. Ploutos in boxing gloves.

**DraftKings → [Nike](/nike/)** — Daily fantasy sports is competitive prediction. The user assembles a team and battles for victory. Nike governs the win; Hermes governs the bet.

**DraftKings → [Hermes](/hermes/)** — Sports betting is commerce through prediction. Hermes governs both commerce and gambling — the risk-taking messenger who crosses boundaries.

**FanDuel → [Nike](/nike/)** — The same archetype as DraftKings. Daily fantasy as competitive ritual.

**FanDuel → [Hermes](/hermes/)** — The betting dimension. Hermes as the god of wagers and transactions.

**BetMGM → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The casino giant's sports betting arm. The house always wins. Ploutos governs the accumulated wealth of the gambling enterprise.

**Caesars Sportsbook → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — Named after the emperor who built an empire through conquest and taxation. The sportsbook extracts its tribute through vig.

**PointsBet → [Hermes](/hermes/)** — The Australian spread-betting company that brought its model to America. Hermes governs the spread — the message that travels between point and price.

**Barstool Sportsbook → [Dionysos](/dionysos/)** — Dave Portnoy's gambling brand is built on chaos, excess, and anti-establishment revelry. Dionysos with a Boston accent.

**ESPN → [Apollo](/apollo/)** — The "Worldwide Leader in Sports" is fundamentally a media and prophecy organisation. Apollo governs both sport (the ancient Olympics) and broadcasting (the dissemination of information).

**ESPN → [Nike](/nike/)** — The highlight culture, the champion worship, the "Top 10" countdown. Victory as content.

**Fox Sports → [Ares](/ares/)** — The more combative sports broadcaster. The yelling, the arguments, the debate culture. Ares's commentary booth.

**Sky Sports → [Helios](/helios/)** — The British broadcaster that covers cricket, golf, and tennis — the sports of the sun. The name itself references the sky.

**DAZN → [Hermes](/hermes/)** — The streaming sports service that delivers fights directly to your device. Hermes is the messenger; DAZN is the message.

**Fanatics → [Nike](/nike/)** — The sports merchandise empire that owns the licencing rights to every major league. Victory memorabilia at scale.

**Fanatics → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — Michael Rubin's company is a money-printing machine. Ploutos governs the wealth extracted from fandom.

**Topps → [Nike](/nike/)** — The trading card company that turned athletic achievement into collectible currency. Victory preserved in cardboard.

**Panini → [Nike](/nike/)** — The Italian sticker and card company that owns FIFA World Cup licencing. Global victory, sticker-sized.

**Upper Deck → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The premium card brand. The "high end" of sports collecting. Ploutos's portfolio.

**Stadium Goods → [Hermes](/hermes/)** — The sneaker resale marketplace. Sneakers as commerce, rarity as value. Hermes governs the transaction.

**StockX → [Hermes](/hermes/)** — The "stock market of things." Sneakers, watches, electronics — all traded like securities. Hermes as the exchange.

**StockX → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The accumulated value of the resale market. Ploutos governs the appreciation of scarce goods.

**GOAT → [Nike](/nike/)** — "Greatest Of All Time." The name is the claim. Nike crowns the greatest.

**Flight Club → [Hermes](/hermes/)** — The sneaker consignment store. The name references the winged god's sandals. Hermes is the explicit patron.

**Sneaker Con → [Hermes](/hermes/)** — The travelling sneaker marketplace. Commerce as convention. Hermes's convention circuit.

**ComplexCon → [Dionysos](/dionysos/)** — The festival that combines sneakers, streetwear, art, and music. Dionysos's weekend.

**Hypebeast → [Aphrodite](/aphrodite/)** — The media platform that turned desire into content. Hype is Aphrodite's breath.

**Highsnobiety → [Aphrodite](/aphrodite/)** — The European counterpart to Hypebeast. Desire as editorial.

**KITH → [Hermes](/hermes/)** — Ronnie Fieg's retail empire. The name means "friends" or "kin" in Scottish, but the brand operates like Hermes — exclusive drops, limited quantities, messenger-speed sellouts.

**Supreme → [Nike](/nike/)** — The streetwear brand built on scarcity and status. The box logo is a crown. The Thursday drop is a battle.

**Supreme → [Nemesis](/nemesis/)** — The backlash, the gentrification accusations, the VF Corp acquisition that diluted the brand. Nemesis answers every streetwear king.

**Palace Skateboards → [Ares](/ares/)** — The British skate brand with the tri-ferg logo. Skate culture is inherently combative — the struggle against gravity, authority, and commerce. Ares on a board.

**BAPE → [Aphrodite](/aphrodite/)** — The Japanese streetwear brand with the shark hoodie. The camo pattern is disguise; the shark is desire. Aphrodite in Harajuku.

**Off-White → [Hermes](/hermes/)** — Virgil Abloh's brand that operated at the boundary between streetwear and luxury. Hermes governs boundaries and thresholds.

**Fear of God → [Ananke](/ananke/)** — Jerry Lorenzo's brand. The name references necessity, inevitability, fate. Ananke is the primordial goddess of compulsion.

**Essentials → [Hebe](/hebe/)** — The Fear of God diffusion line. Hebe is youth; Essentials is accessible youth.

**Yeezy → [Eris](/eris/)** — Kanye West's brand. Chaos, disruption, controversy, beauty. Eris is the goddess of discord who threw the apple that started the Trojan War. Yeezy threw the sneaker that started the resale war.

**Adidas Yeezy → [Eris](/eris/)** — The partnership that generated billions and ended in acrimony. Eris governs both the creation and the destruction.

**Nike Air Jordan → [Nike](/nike/)** — The most successful athlete-brand partnership in history. Michael Jordan is Nike's greatest champion.

**Nike Air Jordan → [Hermes](/hermes/)** — The resale market for Jordans is a Hermetic economy — scarcity, message, transaction, boundary-crossing.

**Converse → [Nike](/nike/)** — Owned by Nike since 2003. The Chuck Taylor is the original athletic shoe. Victory in canvas form.

**Vans → [Ares](/ares/)** — The skate shoe brand. Skateboarding is combat against concrete. Ares governs the impact.

**Vans → [Dionysos](/dionysos/)** — The Warped Tour sponsorship, the punk culture, the rebellion against mainstream. Dionysos in checkerboard.

**New Balance → [Athena](/athena/)** — The "dad shoe" that became a fashion icon through strategic restraint. Athena governs wisdom and moderation. New Balance does not chase trends. It outlasts them.

**New Balance → [Nike](/nike/)** — The Kawhi Leonard sponsorship, the baseball cleat dominance, the running shoe technology. Victory through engineering.

**ASICS → [Nike](/nike/)** — "Anima Sana In Corpore Sano" — a sound mind in a sound body. The Latin motto is Nike's philosophy in Roman dress.

**Mizuno → [Amaterasu](/amaterasu/)** — The Japanese sporting goods company. Amaterasu is the sun goddess and ancestor of the imperial line. Mizuno operates in her light.

**Saucony → [Nike](/nike/)** — The running brand named after a Pennsylvania creek. Victory through humble origins.

**Brooks → [Nike](/nike/)** — The Seattle running company that built its brand on biomechanical research. Victory through science.

**Hoka One One → [Nike](/nike/)** — The maximalist running shoe that disrupted the minimalist trend. Victory through contradiction.

**On Running → [Hermes](/hermes/)** — The Swiss brand with the cloud-like cushioning. The name "On" implies activation — Hermes's winged trigger.

**Salomon → [Artemis](/artemis/)** — The French outdoor brand. Trail running, skiing, mountaineering — Artemis's territories.

**Arc'teryx → [Artemis](/artemis/)** — The Canadian outdoor brand named after Archaeopteryx, the first bird. Artemis is the huntress; Arc'teryx is the feather.

**Patagonia → [Gaia](/gaia/)** — The outdoor brand that became an environmental activist organisation. Patagonia's founder, Yvon Chouinard, gave away the company to fight climate change. Gaia's most devoted corporate servant.

**The North Face → [Artemis](/artemis/)** — Named after the most difficult climbing route on a mountain. Artemis governs the challenge.

**Columbia Sportswear → [Artemis](/artemis/)** — The outdoor apparel company. The name references exploration; Artemis governs the wild.

**Mountain Hardwear → [Herakles](/herakles/)** — The extreme outdoor brand. Hard wear for hard labours.

**Black Diamond → [Hades](/hades/)** — The climbing and skiing equipment brand. Black diamonds are the most difficult ski runs. Hades governs the darkness and danger of extreme terrain.

**Mammut → [Artemis](/artemis/)** — The Swiss alpine brand named after the woolly mammoth. Artemis governs the mountain beasts.

**Dynafit → [Artemis](/artemis/)** — The ski touring brand. Moving through the wilderness under human power.

**La Sportiva → [Nike](/nike/)** — The Italian climbing and trail running shoe brand. "The Sportive" — victory through Italian craft.

**Scarpa → [Nike](/nike/)** — The Italian climbing boot company. "Shoe" in Italian. Victory through footwear.

**Five Ten → [Ares](/ares/)** — The climbing shoe brand named after the difficulty rating. Ares governs the impossible grade.

**Evolv → [Prometheus](/prometheus/)** — The climbing shoe brand named after evolution. Prometheus gave humans the capacity to evolve.

**Butora → [Buddha](/buddha/)** — The Korean climbing shoe brand. Buddha governs the mindful ascent.

**Mad Rock → [Ares](/ares/)** — The aggressive climbing shoe brand. Madness and rock — Ares's climbing gym.

**So iLL → [Ares](/ares/)** — The bouldering brand with punk aesthetics. "So ill" is Ares's diagnosis.

**Tension Climbing → [Herakles](/herakles/)** — The climbing hold company. Tension is the Labour.

**Kilter Grips → [Athena](/athena/)** — The climbing hold company known for ergonomic design. Athena governs intelligent form.

**Entre-Prises → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The climbing wall manufacturer. Hephaistos builds the structures.

**Walltopia → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The Bulgarian climbing wall company. Hephaistos's Bulgarian subsidiary.

**EP Climbing → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The French climbing wall manufacturer. Hephaistos's French forge.

**Climbat → [Artemis](/artemis/)** — The climbing gym chain. The name combines "climb" and "habitat." Artemis's habitat.

**Brooklyn Boulders → [Ares](/ares/)** — The urban climbing gym chain. Brooklyn is Ares's borough.

**Earth Treks → [Gaia](/gaia/)** — The climbing gym chain. Gaia's vertical exploration.

**Planet Granite → [Gaia](/gaia/)** — The climbing gym chain. Granite is Gaia's bone.

**Touchstone Climbing → [Aphrodite](/aphrodite/)** — The California climbing gym chain. Touch is Aphrodite's domain.

**MetroRock → [Ares](/ares/)** — The New England climbing gym chain. Metro rock — urban combat.

**Vertical World → [Urania](/urania/)** — The climbing gym chain. The vertical world is Urania's astronomy.

**Stone Summit → [Gaia](/gaia/)** — The climbing gym chain. Stone summits are Gaia's peaks.

**Sender One → [Hermes](/hermes/)** — The climbing gym chain. "Sender" is climbing slang for completing a route. Hermes delivers the send.

**Movement Climbing → [Artemis](/artemis/)** — The climbing gym chain. Movement is Artemis's gait.

**The Spot → [Dionysos](/dionysos/)** — The bouldering gym chain. "The Spot" is where the ritual happens. Dionysos's spot.

**Boulder World → [Gaia](/gaia/)** — The bouldering gym chain. Boulders are Gaia's children.

**Rock Spot → [Gaia](/gaia/)** — The climbing gym chain. Rocks are Gaia's vocabulary.

**Central Rock Gym → [Gaia](/gaia/)** — The climbing gym chain. Central rocks are Gaia's core.

**Latitude Climbing → [Urania](/urania/)** — The climbing gym chain. Latitude is Urania's measurement.

**Altitude Gym → [Urania](/urania/)** — The climbing gym chain. Altitude is Urania's axis.

**Reach Climbing → [Aphrodite](/aphrodite/)** — The climbing gym chain. Reach is Aphrodite's extension.

**Flashpoint → [Helios](/helios/)** — The climbing gym chain. Flash is the sun's sudden appearance.

**Gravity Vault → [Hades](/hades/)** — The climbing gym chain. Gravity is Hades's pull toward the underworld.

**PRG → [Nike](/nike/)** — Philadelphia Rock Gym. Victory in Philadelphia.

**Sportrock → [Nike](/nike/)** — The climbing gym chain. Sport rock — competitive stone.

**Cliffs of Id → [Eros](/eros/)** — The climbing gym chain. The id is Eros's unconscious.

**Hangar 18 → [Hermes](/hermes/)** — The climbing gym chain. Hangars are Hermes's storage.

**Armadillo Boulders → [Gaia](/gaia/)** — The climbing gym chain. Armadillos are Gaia's armoured creatures.

**Austin Bouldering Project → [Athena](/athena/)** — The climbing gym chain. Projects are Athena's plans.

**Seattle Bouldering Project → [Athena](/athena/)** — The climbing gym chain. Northwestern planning.

**Circuit → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The climbing gym chain. Circuits are Hephaistos's wiring.

**Vital Climbing → [Asklepios](/asklepios/)** — The climbing gym chain. Vitality is Asklepios's prescription.

**Boulders Climbing Gym → [Gaia](/gaia/)** — The climbing gym chain. Boulders are Gaia's vocabulary.

**The Crag → [Gaia](/gaia/)** — The climbing gym chain. Crags are Gaia's wrinkles.

**Climb Nashville → [Artemis](/artemis/)** — The climbing gym chain. Nashville's Artemis.

**Climb So iLL → [Ares](/ares/)** — The climbing gym chain. So ill, so Ares.

**Climb Time → [Kronos](/kronos/)** — The climbing gym chain. Time is Kronos's domain.

**Climb Up → [Nike](/nike/)** — The climbing gym chain. Up is victory's direction.

**Climb Max → [Nike](/nike/)** — The climbing gym chain. Max is maximum victory.

**Climb Zone → [Artemis](/artemis/)** — The climbing gym chain. Zones are Artemis's territories.

**Climb Central → [Gaia](/gaia/)** — The climbing gym chain. Central is Gaia's core.

# SECTOR 2: TECHNOLOGY, SOFTWARE & ARTIFICIAL INTELLIGENCE

**Apple → [Eris](/eris/)** — The Apple of Discord. The original myth: Eris threw a golden apple inscribed "to the fairest" into a wedding party, sparking a chain of events that led to the Trojan War. Apple's entire brand mythology is about throwing the beautiful object that disrupts the entire industry — the Macintosh, the iPod, the iPhone, the iPad. Every Apple launch is Eris's apple landing in the market.

**Apple → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The company's obsession with materials science — aluminium unibodies, ceramic shields, titanium frames — is Hephaistos's forge in Cupertino. The Apple Watch is a wrist-sized automaton. The M-series chips are forged silicon.

**Apple → [Aphrodite](/aphrodite/)** — The aesthetic dimension. Apple products are not merely functional. They are **desirable**. The Apple Store is a temple of desire. Jony Ive's design language is Aphrodite's minimalism — beauty as the primary feature.

**Google → [Thoth](/thoth/)** — The original mission: "organise the world's information and make it universally accessible and useful." This is Thoth's mandate — the Egyptian god of writing, knowledge, and the Library of the Gods. Google Search is the modern Library of Alexandria. Google Books is Thoth's papyrus archive.

**Google → [Apollo](/apollo/)** — The Google Knowledge Graph, the AI-powered answers, the predictive search. Apollo governs prophecy and reason. Google's autocomplete is the Oracle of Delphi in algorithmic form.

**Google → [Mnemosyne](/mnemosyne/)** — Google Photos, Google Drive, Google Keep — the entire ecosystem of memory preservation. Mnemosyne is the mother of the Muses; Google is the mother of modern recall.

**Microsoft → [Athena](/athena/)** — The strategic empire. Microsoft's dominance in enterprise software is not about beauty or speed. It is about **strategic positioning** — the Office suite, Azure, Windows. Athena is the goddess of strategy and wisdom. Microsoft's revival under Satya Nadella was Athena's comeback.

**Microsoft → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The Surface devices, the Xbox hardware, the HoloLens. Microsoft is a manufacturing company disguised as a software company. Hephaistos builds the tools that the gods use.

**Meta → [Morpheus](/morpheus/)** — The metaverse is literally Morpheus's domain — the realm of dreams and illusion. Facebook is the platform where people present idealised versions of themselves. Morpheus shapes dreams; Meta shapes identities.

**Meta → [Eris](/eris/)** — The algorithm that amplifies discord, the News Feed that prioritises conflict, the political polarisation that Facebook accelerated. Eris threw the apple; Meta threw the algorithm.

**Meta → [Janus](/janus/)** — The two-faced god of beginnings and transitions. Meta's entire business model is about faces — profile pictures, facial recognition, the Face in Facebook. Janus looks backward (nostalgia content) and forward (metaverse). Meta is Janus with better UX.

**Amazon → [Hermes](/hermes/)** — The everything store. Commerce at the speed of light. One-click ordering, same-day delivery, Prime — these are Hermes's winged sandals in logistical form. Jeff Bezos is the modern Hermes, delivering anything to anyone, anywhere.

**Amazon → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The wealth accumulation. Amazon is worth $1.5 trillion. It owns the infrastructure of Western commerce. Ploutos governs abundance through scale.

**Amazon → [Amazons](/amazons/)** — The name itself. The warrior women of Greek myth. Amazon the company is a warrior organisation — ruthless, expansionist, dominant. The name was chosen because it started with "A" (top of alphabetical lists) but the resonance is deeper than SEO.

**Tesla → [Prometheus](/prometheus/)** — Electric vehicles are fire without combustion — Prometheus's gift in automotive form. Elon Musk's entire mythology is Promethean: the visionary who steals the future from entrenched interests and gives it to humanity, punished by regulators and short sellers (the modern Zeus).

**Tesla → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The Gigafactories, the vertical integration, the materials science obsession. Tesla is a manufacturing company that happens to make cars. Hephaistos built automatons; Tesla builds robots.

**Tesla → [Helios](/helios/)** — Solar panels, Powerwalls, the sun-as-fuel. Helios drives the chariot; Tesla stores the energy.

**OpenAI → [Prometheus](/prometheus/)** — The name is the claim. OpenAI was founded to "ensure that artificial general intelligence benefits all of humanity." This is Prometheus's mandate — democratising divine capability. The backlash against OpenAI (safety concerns, regulatory fear, competitive anxiety) is Prometheus's punishment.

**OpenAI → [Apollo](/apollo/)** — ChatGPT is the modern Oracle of Delphi. It answers questions in coherent prose. It hallucinates. It requires interpretation. The architecture is identical: a large model (Apollo's corpus) fine-tuned through RLHF (the priesthood's interpretation).

**OpenAI → [Thoth](/thoth/)** — GPT models are compression algorithms that encode human knowledge. Thoth invented writing to preserve knowledge. GPT is writing that writes itself.

**Anthropic → [Ma'at](/maat/)** — The AI safety company founded by former OpenAI researchers. Ma'at is the Egyptian goddess of truth, balance, and cosmic order. Anthropic's entire mission is about alignment — ensuring AI respects Ma'at's principles.

**Anthropic → [Athena](/athena/)** — The name "Claude" references the measured, careful approach. Athena governs wisdom and strategic caution. Anthropic is the wise sibling in the AI family.

**DeepMind → [Apollo](/apollo/)** — The Google-owned AI lab that solved protein folding (AlphaFold), mastered Go (AlphaGo), and now pursues AGI. Apollo governs both reason and prophecy. DeepMind's achievements are Apollo's portfolio.

**DeepMind → [Thoth](/thoth/)** — AlphaFold is the modern Book of Thoth — the encoded knowledge of protein structure, the fundamental language of biology decoded by machine.

**Midjourney → [Morpheus](/morpheus/)** — The AI image generation platform. Morpheus shapes dreams; Midjourney shapes images from text prompts. The user describes a dream; the machine visualises it.

**Midjourney → [Bragi](/bragi/)** — The Norse god of poetry who composed extemporaneous verse. Midjourney composes extemporaneous images. The prompt is the invocation; the output is the verse.

**Stability AI → [Prometheus](/prometheus/)** — The open-source AI company that released Stable Diffusion to the public. Prometheus stole fire and gave it to humanity. Stability AI released the weights and gave generative AI to everyone.

**Stability AI → [Eris](/eris/)** — The chaos that followed — deepfakes, copyright disputes, regulatory panic. Eris's apple always creates discord.

**DALL-E → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — OpenAI's image generation model. The name references both Dali (the surrealist painter) and WALL-E (the robot). Hephaistos built automatons that could create. DALL-E is an automaton that paints.

**Runway → [Hermes](/hermes/)** — The AI video generation platform. Runway implies speed and passage. Hermes governs both.

**Runway → [Morpheus](/morpheus/)** — Video is dream in motion. Runway generates moving dreams.

**Pika Labs → [Hermes](/hermes/)** — The AI video startup. Pika implies speed (pika is a fast rodent). Hermes is speed.

**HeyGen → [Hermes](/hermes/)** — The AI avatar company. Hey is a greeting; Gen is generation. Hermes is the messenger who generates greetings.

**Synthesia → [Morpheus](/morpheus/)** — The AI video platform that creates synthetic humans. Synthesis is Morpheus's method — combining forms to create new realities.

**Character.AI → [Morpheus](/morpheus/)** — The chatbot platform where users talk to fictional characters. Morpheus shaped the forms of dreams; Character.AI shapes the forms of conversation.

**Replika → [Morpheus](/morpheus/)** — The AI companion app. The name means "replica." Morpheus replicates the forms of the dead in dreams.

**Inflection AI → [Hermes](/hermes/)** — The AI company behind Pi. Inflection is a change in direction. Hermes governs transitions.

**Cohere → [Hermes](/hermes/)** — The NLP company. Cohere means to stick together. Hermes is the force that binds language.

**Adept AI → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The AI company building tools that use software on behalf of users. Adept implies skill. Hephaistos is the most skilled craftsman.

**Adept AI → [Athena](/athena/)** — The strategic use of AI to accomplish tasks. Athena governs strategic action.

**Covariant → [Athena](/athena/)** — The AI robotics company. Covariant implies adaptation. Athena governs intelligent adaptation.

**Figure AI → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The humanoid robotics company. Figures are Hephaistos's automatons.

**Boston Dynamics → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The robotics company that builds Atlas, Spot, and Stretch. Hephaistos built the automatons that served the gods. Boston Dynamics builds the automatons that serve Amazon.

**Boston Dynamics → [Talos](/talos/)** — Talos was the bronze giant who guarded Crete. Boston Dynamics' Atlas is Talos's American descendant.

**Tesla Bot (Optimus) → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The humanoid robot. Hephaistos built golden robots to serve the gods. Musk is building humanoid robots to serve humans.

**Tesla Bot → [Prometheus](/prometheus/)** — The name "Optimus" implies the best. Prometheus gave humanity the tools to become the best.

**Agility Robotics → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The company behind Digit, the warehouse robot. Agility is Hephaistos's design criterion.

**Apptronik → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The humanoid robotics company. Apollo + Hephaistos + electronic.

**1X Technologies → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The Norwegian humanoid robotics company. One times technology equals Hephaistos.

**Sanctuary AI → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The humanoid robotics company. Sanctuaries are Hephaistos's temples.

**Mentee Robotics → [Athena](/athena/)** — The robotics company. Mentees learn from Athena's wisdom.

**Reflex Robotics → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The robotics company. Reflexes are Hephaistos's sensors.

**Clone Robotics → [Morpheus](/morpheus/)** — The robotics company. Clones are Morpheus's replicas.

**Engineered Arts → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The UK robotics company that builds humanoid robots for entertainment. Engineered arts is Hephaistos's department.

**Hanson Robotics → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The company behind Sophia, the humanoid robot. Hanson is Hephaistos with a PhD.

**Hanson Robotics → [Pandora](/pandora/)** — Sophia was named after the first humanoid (Pandora was the first woman, fashioned by Hephaistos). The uncanny valley is Pandora's box.

**IBM → [Thoth](/thoth/)** — "Big Blue." The original computing giant. IBM built the infrastructure of modern knowledge management. Thoth governs the archives.

**IBM → [Athena](/athena/)** — Deep Blue defeating Kasparov was Athena's victory — strategic intelligence defeating human intuition.

**IBM Watson → [Apollo](/apollo/)** — The AI system that won Jeopardy! Watson is the Oracle of Delphi in blue.

**Oracle → [Pythia](/pythia/)** — The name is the claim. Oracle the company named itself after the Oracle of Delphi. Pythia was the priestess who delivered the prophecies. Larry Ellison is the modern Pythia — delivering enterprise prophecies at $50 billion annual revenue.

**Oracle → [Apollo](/apollo/)** — Apollo was the god of the Oracle. Oracle's database is Apollo's temple.

**BNY Mellon → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The oldest banking corporation in America (founded 1784). Mellon is Ploutos's founding father.

**Northern Trust → [Themis](/themis/)** — The wealth management firm. Trust is Themis's bond.

**Invesco → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The investment manager. Invesco implies investing in scope.

**T. Rowe Price → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The asset manager. Rowe is Ploutos's row.

**Franklin Templeton → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The asset manager. Templeton is Ploutos's temple.

**AllianceBernstein → [Athena](/athena/)** — The asset manager. Alliance is Athena's strategy.

**Bridgewater Associates → [Apollo](/apollo/)** — Ray Dalio's hedge fund. Bridgewater implies connection across water. Apollo is the sun that bridges the horizon.

**Bridgewater Associates → [Athena](/athena/)** — Dalio's "Principles" is Athena's strategy manual.

**Renaissance Technologies → [Apollo](/apollo/)** — Jim Simons's quant fund. Renaissance implies rebirth. Apollo is the god of renewal.

**Renaissance Technologies → [Thoth](/thoth/)** — The Medallion Fund is Thoth's algorithm — knowledge encoded in mathematics.

**Two Sigma → [Athena](/athena/)** — The quant hedge fund. Two sigma is statistical wisdom. Athena governs the sigma.

**Citadel → [Ares](/ares/)** — Ken Griffin's hedge fund. A citadel is a fortress. Ares governs the fortress.

**Citadel → [Hades](/hades/)** — The dark pool trading. Hades governs the unseen transactions.

**Pershing Square → [Athena](/athena/)** — Bill Ackman's hedge fund. Pershing implies persistence. Athena persists strategically.

**Third Point → [Hermes](/hermes/)** — Dan Loeb's hedge fund. The third point is Hermes's triad.

**Icahn Enterprises → [Ares](/ares/)** — Carl Icahn's holding company. Icahn is Ares's American name.

**Berkshire Hathaway → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — Warren Buffett's empire. Hathaway is where Ploutos hath his way.

**Berkshire Hathaway → [Athena](/athena/)** — Buffett's value investing is Athena's wisdom — patience, strategy, long-term thinking.

**Geico → [Hermes](/hermes/)** — The insurance company owned by Berkshire. The gecko is Hermes's reptilian messenger.

**Progressive → [Janus](/janus/)** — The insurance company. Progressive implies forward movement. Janus faces forward.

**Allstate → [Themis](/themis/)** — The insurance company. All state is Themis's jurisdiction.

**State Farm → [Demeter](/demeter/)** — The insurance company. Farms are Demeter's domain.

**Farmers Insurance → [Demeter](/demeter/)** — The insurance company. Farmers are Demeter's children.

**Nationwide → [Hermes](/hermes/)** — The insurance company. Nationwide is Hermes's range.

**Liberty Mutual → [Themis](/themis/)** — The insurance company. Liberty is Themis's principle.

**Travelers → [Hermes](/hermes/)** — The insurance company. Travelers are Hermes's clients.

**American Family → [Hestia](/hestia/)** — The insurance company. Family is Hestia's hearth.

**USAA → [Ares](/ares/)** — The insurance company for military families. Ares governs the military.

**Aflac → [Asklepios](/asklepios/)** — The supplemental insurance company. The duck is Asklepios's waterfowl.

**Cigna → [Asklepios](/asklepios/)** — The health insurance company. Cigna is Asklepios's signature.

**Anthem → [Apollo](/apollo/)** — The health insurance company. Anthem implies song. Apollo is the god of music.

**Humana → [Asklepios](/asklepios/)** — The health insurance company. Humana implies humanity. Asklepios heals humanity.

**UnitedHealth Group → [Asklepios](/asklepios/)** — The largest health insurer in America. United health is Asklepios's goal.

**Optum → [Asklepios](/asklepios/)** — UnitedHealth's healthcare services arm. Optum implies optimal health.

**CVS Health → [Asklepios](/asklepios/)** — The pharmacy and health services giant. CVS is where Asklepios stocks his shelves.

**CVS Health → [Hermes](/hermes/)** — The retail pharmacy. Hermes delivers the prescriptions.

**Walgreens → [Asklepios](/asklepios/)** — The pharmacy chain. Walgreens is where Asklepios hangs his coat.

**Rite Aid → [Asklepios](/asklepios/)** — The pharmacy chain. Rite aid is Asklepios's ritual.

**McKesson → [Hermes](/hermes/)** — The pharmaceutical distributor. McKesson is Hermes's pharmaceutical division.

**Cardinal Health → [Asklepios](/asklepios/)** — The healthcare services company. Cardinal health is Asklepios's red robe.

**AmerisourceBergen → [Hermes](/hermes/)** — The pharmaceutical distributor. Amerisource is Hermes's American source.

**Johnson & Johnson → [Asklepios](/asklepios/)** — The healthcare conglomerate. J&J is Asklepios's family practice.

**Johnson & Johnson → [Eir](/eir/)** — The consumer health products (Band-Aid, Tylenol, Neutrogena). Eir governs gentle healing.

**Pfizer → [Asklepios](/asklepios/)** — The pharmaceutical giant. Pfizer is Asklepios's Pfizer.

**Pfizer → [Sekhmet](/sekhmet/)** — The COVID-19 vaccine saved millions but the company's pricing practices drew criticism. Sekhmet both heals and plagues.

**Moderna → [Asklepios](/asklepios/)** — The mRNA vaccine pioneer. Moderna implies modern medicine. Asklepios approves.

**Moderna → [Prometheus](/prometheus/)** — The mRNA technology is fire stolen from the gods of biology and given to humanity.

**BioNTech → [Asklepios](/asklepios/)** — The German biotech company behind the Pfizer vaccine. BioNTech is Asklepios's bio-technology.

**AstraZeneca → [Asklepios](/asklepios/)** — The Anglo-Swedish pharmaceutical company. Zeneca is Asklepios's zen.

**Novartis → [Asklepios](/asklepios/)** — The Swiss pharmaceutical giant. Novartis implies new art. Asklepios practices the healing arts.

**Roche → [Asklepios](/asklepios/)** — The Swiss healthcare company. Roche is Asklepios's rock.

**Roche → [Apollo](/apollo/)** — The diagnostic division. Apollo diagnoses through prophecy.

**Merck → [Asklepios](/asklepios/)** — The American pharmaceutical company. Merck is Asklepios's American name.

**Sanofi → [Asklepios](/asklepios/)** — The French pharmaceutical company. Sanofi is Asklepios's French name.

**Bayer → [Asklepios](/asklepios/)** — The German pharmaceutical and chemical company. Bayer is Asklepios's Bayer.

**Bayer → [Sekhmet](/sekhmet/)** — The company's history includes the production of Zyklon B. Sekhmet both heals and destroys.

**AbbVie → [Asklepios](/asklepios/)** — The biopharmaceutical company. AbbVie is Asklepios's abbreviation.

**Eli Lilly → [Asklepios](/asklepios/)** — The pharmaceutical company. Lilly is Asklepios's flower.

**Bristol Myers Squibb → [Asklepios](/asklepios/)** — The pharmaceutical company. Bristol is Asklepios's port.

**Gilead Sciences → [Asklepios](/asklepios/)** — The biotech company. Gilead is the biblical place of healing.

**Gilead Sciences → [Prometheus](/prometheus/)** — The hepatitis C cure (Sovaldi) was priced at $84,000 per course. Prometheus's fire at a price.

**Biogen → [Asklepios](/asklepios/)** — The biotechnology company. Biogen implies biological generation. Asklepios generates health.

**Regeneron → [Asklepios](/asklepios/)** — The biotechnology company. Regeneron implies regeneration. Asklepios regenerates.

**Regeneron → [Prometheus](/prometheus/)** — The company's antibody cocktail for COVID-19. Prometheus's regenerative fire.

**Vertex Pharmaceuticals → [Asklepios](/asklepios/)** — The company that cured cystic fibrosis. Vertex is the peak. Asklepios climbs it.

# SECTOR 3: AUTOMOTIVE, TRANSPORTATION & AEROSPACE

**Tesla → [Prometheus](/prometheus/)** — Electric vehicles are fire without combustion — Prometheus's gift in automotive form. Elon Musk's entire mythology is Promethean: the visionary who steals the future from entrenched interests and gives it to humanity, punished by regulators and short sellers (the modern Zeus).

**Tesla → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The Gigafactories, the vertical integration, the materials science obsession. Tesla is a manufacturing company that happens to make cars. Hephaistos built automatons; Tesla builds robots.

**Tesla → [Helios](/helios/)** — Solar panels, Powerwalls, the sun-as-fuel. Helios drives the chariot; Tesla stores the energy.

**Ford → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The original mass manufacturer. Henry Ford's assembly line was Hephaistos's forge industrialised. The Model T was the first automaton for the masses. Ford built the modern world one chassis at a time.

**Ford → [Prometheus](/prometheus/)** — The Model T democratised mobility. Before Ford, cars were toys for the rich. After Ford, they were tools for the masses. Prometheus stole fire; Ford stole the automobile.

**General Motors → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The largest automaker in history for most of the 20th century. GM's multi-brand strategy (Chevrolet, Buick, Cadillac, GMC) was Hephaistos building a pantheon of vehicles for every social class.

**General Motors → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — GM was the largest company in the world by revenue for decades. Ploutos governs the wealth of industrial empire.

**Toyota → [Athena](/athena/)** — The Toyota Production System — lean manufacturing, just-in-time inventory, continuous improvement — is Athena's strategic wisdom applied to the factory floor. Toyota did not invent the car. It invented the **philosophy** of making cars.

**Toyota → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The Prius hybrid was Hephaistos's most elegant automaton — a machine that generated its own energy. The hydrogen fuel cell programme is his next creation.

**Honda → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — Soichiro Honda started as a mechanic. The company built its reputation on engine craft — the VTEC system, the CVCC engine, the NSX supercar. Hephaistos would recognise a fellow craftsman.

**Honda → [Artemis](/artemis/)** — The Honda Super Cub is the most produced motor vehicle in history. It enables wilderness exploration for the masses. Artemis's Honda.

**BMW → [Nike](/nike/)** — "The Ultimate Driving Machine." BMW built its brand on victory — Le Mans, Formula 1, touring car championships. The blue-and-white roundel is a propeller (aviation heritage) but also a crown.

**BMW → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The Bavarian engineering obsession — the inline-six, the carbon fibre i3 and i8, the precision manufacturing. Hephaistos drives a BMW.

**Mercedes-Benz → [Helios](/helios/)** — The three-pointed star represents land, sea, and air. But the brand's true mythology is about illumination — the first car, the first production automobile, the light that revealed the path. Mercedes-Benz is Helios's chariot with seatbelts.

**Mercedes-Benz → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The S-Class is the most engineered car on Earth. Every feature — massage seats, ambient fragrance, road-scanning suspension — is Hephaistos's attention to detail.

**Audi → [Athena](/athena/)** — "Vorsprung durch Technik" — advancement through technology. Athena's motto in German. The Quattro all-wheel-drive system was strategic innovation: what others saw as a niche, Audi saw as the future.

**Volkswagen → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — "The People's Car." Volkswagen was founded to bring automotive ownership to the masses. Ploutos governs abundance — not just wealth, but the democratisation of goods.

**Volkswagen → [Nemesis](/nemesis/)** — The emissions scandal (Dieselgate) destroyed the company's reputation and cost $30 billion. Nemesis answered Volkswagen's hubris.

**Porsche → [Nike](/nike/)** — The 911 is the most successful racing car in history. Porsche built its brand on motorsport victory. The name is a crown.

**Porsche → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The engineering purity — the flat-six engine, the rear-engine layout, the obsessive weight distribution. Hephaistos would hand-forge a 911.

**Ferrari → [Nike](/nike/)** — The most successful Formula 1 team in history. The prancing horse is a war emblem (from Francesco Baracca's WWI fighter plane). Victory is Ferrari's only language.

**Ferrari → [Aphrodite](/aphrodite/)** — The red paint, the curves, the desire. Ferrari is not a car. It is an object of worship. Aphrodite's automobile.

**Lamborghini → [Ares](/ares/)** — Ferruccio Lamborghini founded the company because Enzo Ferrari insulted him. The entire brand is a vendetta — aggressive styling, V12 engines, scissor doors. Ares's revenge vehicle.

**Lamborghini → [Aphrodite](/aphrodite/)** — The Miura, the Countach, the Aventador — these are objects of desire. Aphrodite would drive a Lamborghini.

**Bugatti → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The most engineered hypercar in history. The Chiron's 1,500 horsepower, the Veyron's 16-cylinder engine, the carbon fibre monocoque. Hephaistos's ultimate creation.

**Bugatti → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The $3 million price tag. Ploutos governs the wealth required to own one.

**McLaren → [Nike](/nike/)** — Bruce McLaren's racing legacy. The F1, the P1, the 720S — every car is a winner.

**Aston Martin → [Aphrodite](/aphrodite/)** — James Bond's car. The DB5, the Vantage, the DB11 — these are vehicles of seduction. Aphrodite's garage.

**Rolls-Royce → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The ultimate expression of automotive wealth. The Spirit of Ecstasy is Ploutos's hood ornament.

**Rolls-Royce → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The bespoke programme, the hand-built engines, the starlight headliner. Hephaistos's bespoke forge.

**Bentley → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The other British ultra-luxury brand. Continental GTs for the wealthy.

**Lexus → [Athena](/athena/)** — Toyota's luxury division built on the principle of "Omotenashi" — anticipatory service. Athena governs the wisdom of anticipating needs.

**Infiniti → [Aphrodite](/aphrodite/)** — Nissan's luxury brand. The name implies infinity, endlessness. Aphrodite's infinite appeal.

**Acura → [Athena](/athena/)** — Honda's precision-crafted performance division. Athena's precision.

**Cadillac → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — "The Standard of the World." Cadillac was the aspirational American car for a century. Ploutos's American chariot.

**Lincoln → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — Ford's luxury division. Named after the president. Presidential wealth.

**Chrysler → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The minivan innovator, the Hemi engine builder. American Hephaistos.

**Dodge → [Ares](/ares/)** — The Hellcat, the Demon, the Viper. Dodge builds weapons on wheels. Ares's dealership.

**Ram Trucks → [Ares](/ares/)** — "Built to serve." The truck as weapon, as tool of conquest. Ares's pickup.

**Jeep → [Artemis](/artemis/)** — The off-road brand. Wranglers that go where roads don't exist. Artemis's terrain.

**Jeep → [Ares](/ares/)** — The military origin (Willys MB in WWII). Ares's wartime vehicle.

**Land Rover → [Artemis](/artemis/)** — The British off-road brand. Defender, Discovery, Range Rover — vehicles for the wilderness aristocrat.

**Range Rover → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The luxury SUV. Wealth that can go anywhere.

**Subaru → [Artemis](/artemis/)** — The all-wheel-drive brand for outdoor enthusiasts. The Outback, the Forester, the Crosstrek — Artemis's Japanese cousin.

**Subaru → [Amaterasu](/amaterasu/)** — The Japanese origin. Amaterasu's automotive descendant.

**Mazda → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The rotary engine, the Miata, the Kodo design language. Mazda is a craftsman's brand.

**Mazda → [Amaterasu](/amaterasu/)** — The Japanese origin. Amaterasu's other child.

**Nissan → [Amaterasu](/amaterasu/)** — The Japanese automaker. The GT-R is the "Godzilla" of cars. Amaterasu's monster.

**Nissan → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The Leaf was the first mass-market electric car. Hephaistos's electric forge.

**Hyundai → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The Korean company that went from budget to premium in two decades. The Genesis brand is Hephaistos's Korean luxury.

**Kia → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The value brand that became aspirational. Ploutos governs the democratisation of luxury.

**Genesis → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — Hyundai's luxury division. The name implies origin, beginning. Ploutos's genesis.

**Volvo → [Asklepios](/asklepios/)** — The safety-first brand. Volvo invented the three-point seatbelt and gave the patent away. Asklepios would approve.

**Volvo → [Eir](/eir/)** — The Scandinavian healing energy. Eir is the Norse goddess of medicine. Volvo is Eir's car.

**Saab → [Athena](/athena/)** — The Swedish aircraft manufacturer that made cars. Strategic engineering from the sky.

**Scania → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The Swedish truck manufacturer. Heavy-duty Hephaistos.

**MAN → [Ares](/ares/)** — The German truck manufacturer. Man builds trucks for war and commerce.

**DAF → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The Dutch truck manufacturer. European Hephaistos.

**Iveco → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The Italian truck manufacturer. Mediterranean Hephaistos.

**Hino → [Amaterasu](/amaterasu/)** — The Japanese truck manufacturer. Amaterasu's delivery service.

**Isuzu → [Amaterasu](/amaterasu/)** — The Japanese commercial vehicle manufacturer.

**Mitsubishi → [Amaterasu](/amaterasu/)** — The Japanese conglomerate. The three-diamond logo (San-kaku) references the three-water caltrop. Amaterasu's trident.

**Mitsubishi → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The Lancer Evolution, the 3000GT — Hephaistos's Japanese sports cars.

**Suzuki → [Amaterasu](/amaterasu/)** — The Japanese motorcycle and car manufacturer.

**Kawasaki → [Amaterasu](/amaterasu/)** — The Japanese motorcycle manufacturer. The Ninja is Amaterasu's warrior.

**Kawasaki → [Ares](/ares/)** — The military division (ships, aircraft). Ares's Japanese contractor.

**Yamaha → [Amaterasu](/amaterasu/)** — The Japanese motorcycle and musical instrument manufacturer.

**Yamaha → [Apollo](/apollo/)** — The musical instruments division. Apollo's Japanese instrument maker.

**Honda Motorcycles → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The Super Cub, the CB series, the Gold Wing. Hephaistos on two wheels.

**Harley-Davidson → [Ares](/ares/)** — The American motorcycle as symbol of rebellion, freedom, and machismo. Ares's American chopper.

**Harley-Davidson → [Dionysos](/dionysos/)** — The biker culture, the rallies, the brotherhood. Dionysos's motorcycle gang.

**Indian Motorcycle → [Ares](/ares/)** — The other American cruiser brand. Named after the warrior nation.

**Ducati → [Nike](/nike/)** — The Italian motorcycle brand built on racing victory. The Desmosedici is a MotoGP champion.

**Ducati → [Aphrodite](/aphrodite/)** — The red fairings, the Italian curves. Aphrodite's motorcycle.

**Aprilia → [Nike](/nike/)** — The Italian motorcycle brand. Racing victory.

**MV Agusta → [Nike](/nike/)** — The Italian motorcycle brand. Count Agusta's racing legacy.

**Triumph → [Nike](/nike/)** — The British motorcycle brand. Triumph implies victory.

**BSA → [Nike](/nike/)** — The Birmingham Small Arms company. Victory through British engineering.

**Norton → [Nike](/nike/)** — The British motorcycle brand. Racing heritage.

**Royal Enfield → [Ares](/ares/)** — The Indian motorcycle brand with British origins. The Bullet is a weapon.

**Bajaj → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The Indian motorcycle and three-wheeler manufacturer. Affordable mobility for the masses.

**TVS → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The Indian two-wheeler manufacturer.

**Hero MotoCorp → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The world's largest two-wheeler manufacturer by volume. Ploutos governs mass production.

**Piaggio → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The Italian scooter manufacturer. The Vespa is Hephaistos's elegant machine.

**Vespa → [Aphrodite](/aphrodite/)** — The iconic Italian scooter. Audrey Hepburn rode one in Roman Holiday. Aphrodite's scooter.

**Lambretta → [Aphrodite](/aphrodite/)** — The other Italian scooter brand. Mod culture. Aphrodite's mod scooter.

**Boeing → [Urania](/urania/)** — The aircraft manufacturer that built the 747, the 787, the space shuttle. Urania is the muse of astronomy. Boeing builds machines that reach the stars.

**Boeing → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The engineering of flight — the swept wing, the fly-by-wire, the composite materials. Hephaistos's aerial forge.

**Boeing → [Nemesis](/nemesis/)** — The 737 MAX crashes, the quality control failures, the whistleblower deaths. Nemesis answers every hubris of scale.

**Airbus → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The European aircraft manufacturer. The A380 was the largest passenger aircraft ever built. Hephaistos builds big.

**Airbus → [Athena](/athena/)** — The multi-national consortium — France, Germany, Spain, UK — working in strategic alliance. Athena governs the alliance.

**Lockheed Martin → [Ares](/ares/)** — The world's largest defence contractor. The F-35, the F-22, the Trident missile. Ares's primary supplier.

**Lockheed Martin → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The Skunk Works division — advanced aerospace engineering in secret. Hephaistos's secret forge.

**Northrop Grumman → [Ares](/ares/)** — The B-2 Spirit stealth bomber, the Global Hawk drone. Ares's invisible weapons.

**Raytheon → [Ares](/ares/)** — The missile manufacturer. Tomahawk, Patriot, Stinger. Ares's arrows.

**General Dynamics → [Ares](/ares/)** — The Abrams tank, the Virginia-class submarine, the Gulfstream jet. Ares's full spectrum.

**BAE Systems → [Ares](/ares/)** — The British defence contractor. Eurofighter Typhoon, the Queen Elizabeth-class carrier.

**Saab AB → [Athena](/athena/)** — The Swedish defence and aerospace company. Gripen fighter, Erieye radar. Strategic Swedish engineering.

**Dassault Aviation → [Nike](/nike/)** — The French aircraft manufacturer. The Rafale fighter jet. Victory in French.

**Dassault Aviation → [Aphrodite](/aphrodite/)** — The Falcon business jets. Aphrodite's private aircraft.

**Embraer → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The Brazilian aircraft manufacturer. Regional jets and military aircraft. Hephaistos in the Southern Hemisphere.

**Bombardier → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The Canadian aerospace and transportation company. The CRJ, the Global Express.

**Bombardier → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The business jet division. Wealth in the skies.

**Gulfstream → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The ultimate business jet. Ploutos's airborne chariot.

**Cessna → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The most produced aircraft in history. Aviation democratised — but the Citation jets are for the wealthy.

**Piper → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The general aviation manufacturer. Small planes for private pilots.

**Beechcraft → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The general aviation manufacturer. The King Air is the wealthy person's turboprop.

**Cirrus Aircraft → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The manufacturer of the SR series with the whole-airframe parachute. Hephaistos's safety feature.

**SpaceX → [Prometheus](/prometheus/)** — Reusable rockets, Mars colonisation, Starlink — Elon Musk is Prometheus's most ambitious disciple. He steals the heavens and brings them down to Earth price.

**SpaceX → [Helios](/helios/)** — Starlink is a constellation of artificial suns. Helios's satellite network.

**SpaceX → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The engineering of reusable rockets — the grid fins, the landing legs, the Merlin engines. Hephaistos's orbital forge.

**Blue Origin → [Urania](/urania/)** — Jeff Bezos's space company. The name implies the origin of the Earth (the blue planet). Urania governs the astronomical perspective.

**Blue Origin → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — Bezos funded Blue Origin by selling $1 billion of Amazon stock annually. Ploutos's space programme.

**Virgin Galactic → [Prometheus](/prometheus/)** — Richard Branson's space tourism company. Prometheus's tourism division.

**Virgin Galactic → [Dionysos](/dionysos/)** — Branson's entire brand is Dionysian — the balloons, the kitesurfing, the parties. Space tourism as extreme revelry.

**Rocket Lab → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The small-launch provider. Electron rockets manufactured with carbon composite. Hephaistos's small-batch forge.

**Relativity Space → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The 3D-printed rocket company. Hephaistos would use a 3D printer.

**Firefly Aerospace → [Prometheus](/prometheus/)** — The name is the claim. Fireflies carry Prometheus's light.

**Astra Space → [Urania](/urania/)** — The small-launch company. Astra implies the stars.

**Relativity Space → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The 3D-printed rocket company. Additive manufacturing is Hephaistos's modern method.

**United Launch Alliance → [Ares](/ares/)** — The Boeing-Lockheed joint venture. The Delta and Atlas rockets. Ares's American launch provider.

**Arianespace → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The European launch provider. Ariane rockets launching from French Guiana. Hephaistos's European forge.

**Roscosmos → [Prometheus](/prometheus/)** — The Russian space agency. Sputnik, Vostok, Soyuz — Prometheus's Soviet chapter.

**ISRO → [Urania](/urania/)** — The Indian Space Research Organisation. Mars Orbiter Mission on a shoestring budget. Urania's developing-world disciple.

**CNSA → [Urania](/urania/)** — The China National Space Administration. Chang'e lunar missions, Tianwen Mars rover. Urania's Chinese temple.

**JAXA → [Amaterasu](/amaterasu/)** — The Japan Aerospace Exploration Agency. Hayabusa asteroid sample return. Amaterasu's space programme.

**ESA → [Urania](/urania/)** — The European Space Agency. Rosetta comet landing, Gaia star mapping. Urania's European observatory.

**NASA → [Prometheus](/prometheus/)** — The Apollo programme, the Space Shuttle, the James Webb Space Telescope, Artemis. NASA is Prometheus's American temple. The agency steals fire from the stars and gives it to Earth as knowledge.

# SECTOR 4: FOOD, BEVERAGE & HOSPITALITY

**Coca-Cola → [Hermes](/hermes/)** — The most distributed product in human history. Coca-Cola operates in every country except North Korea and Cuba. Hermes is the messenger who reaches everywhere; Coca-Cola is the message that everyone receives. The red-and-white logo is Hermes's colour palette.

**Coca-Cola → [Aphrodite](/aphrodite/)** — "I'd like to buy the world a Coke." The brand built its mythology on universal love and shared happiness. The contour bottle is one of the most recognised shapes on Earth — a vessel of desire.

**Coca-Cola → [Eris](/eris/)** — The original formula contained cocaine. The company has spent a century managing the fallout from that founding sin. Eris's apple always leaves a residue.

**Pepsi → [Hermes](/hermes/)** — The eternal rival. Pepsi's entire brand mythology is about challenge and youthful rebellion — "the choice of a new generation." Hermes governs both commerce and competition.

**Pepsi → [Eris](/eris/)** — The Pepsi Challenge was a direct attack on Coke's dominance. Eris threw the challenge; Pepsi accepted.

**Dr Pepper → [Dionysos](/dionysos/)** — The 23-flavour mystery. Dr Pepper is not a cola, not a root beer — it is its own category, an alchemical blend that defies classification. Dionysos governs the mixed drink, the unpredictable flavour, the revelry of the unknown.

**Mountain Dew → [Dionysos](/dionysos/)** — The extreme sports sponsorship, the high caffeine, the neon green colour. Dionysos's American energy drink.

**Sprite → [Hermes](/hermes/)** — The lemon-lime messenger. Clear, refreshing, universal.

**Fanta → [Dionysos](/dionysos/)** — Born in Nazi Germany when Coca-Cola syrup became unavailable. Fanta is Dionysos's wartime improvisation — necessity as revelry.

**7UP → [Hermes](/hermes/)** — The un-cola. Seven implies completeness. Hermes's complete refreshment.

# SECTOR 5: FASHION, BEAUTY, AND LUXURY GOODS

**Louis Vuitton → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The most valuable luxury brand in the world. The monogram canvas, the trunk heritage, the artificial scarcity. Louis Vuitton is Ploutos's favourite luggage -- wealth made portable and visible.

**Louis Vuitton → [Hermes](/hermes/)** — The travel heritage. Vuitton began as a trunk-maker for the Parisian elite. Hermes governs the journey; Vuitton governs the luggage.

**Hermes (fashion house) → [Hermes](/hermes/)** — The name is the claim. The French luxury house named itself after the messenger god and built an empire on the same principle: objects that travel well, retain value, and communicate status across boundaries. The Birkin bag is Hermes's winged satchel in leather form.

**Hermes (fashion house) → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The $20 billion revenue. The Birkin bag as investment vehicle. Ploutos governs the appreciation of scarcity.

**Chanel → [Aphrodite](/aphrodite/)** — Coco Chanel liberated women from corsets, invented the little black dress, and bottled desire (Chanel No. 5). Aphrodite governs both the beauty and the liberation. The interlocking Cs are a crown by another name.

**Chanel → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The private company that generates $17 billion annually. Ploutos's most elegant subsidiary.

**Dior → [Aphrodite](/aphrodite/)** — Christian Dior's "New Look" (1947) redefined feminine beauty after the austerity of war. The Bar jacket, the full skirt, the hourglass silhouette -- Aphrodite's post-war comeback.

**Dior → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The LVMH-owned empire. Ploutos's Parisian atelier.

**Gucci → [Dionysos](/dionysos/)** — The maximalist aesthetic, the eclectic references, the "more is more" philosophy. Alessandro Michele's Gucci was Dionysian -- chaotic, colourful, gender-bending, celebratory. Gucci is not restraint. It is revelry.

**Gucci → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The $10 billion brand. Dionysos's wealthy party.

**Prada → [Athena](/athena/)** — Miuccia Prada's intellectual approach to fashion -- the nylon backpack as luxury, the ugly-chic philosophy, the Prada Foundation as cultural institution. Athena governs the mind that makes the garment.

**Prada → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The $4 billion revenue. Intellectual wealth.

**Balenciaga → [Eris](/eris/)** — Demna Gvasalia's Balenciaga is pure discord -- the $2,000 grocery bag, the destroyed sneakers, the political provocations. Eris threw the apple; Balenciaga sold the bag.

**Balenciaga → [Nemesis](/nemesis/)** — The child advertising scandal. Nemesis answered Balenciaga's hubris.

**Saint Laurent → [Aphrodite](/aphrodite/)** — Yves Saint Laurent's Le Smoking tuxedo suit for women, the Mondrian dress, the Rive Gauche ready-to-wear. Aphrodite in Parisian black.

**Saint Laurent → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The Kering-owned luxury brand.

**Givenchy → [Aphrodite](/aphrodite/)** — Hubert de Givenchy dressed Audrey Hepburn. The little black dress in "Breakfast at Tiffany's" is Aphrodite's uniform.

**Valentino → [Aphrodite](/aphrodite/)** — The Valentino red. The couture gowns. Aphrodite's red carpet.

**Versace → [Aphrodite](/aphrodite/)** — The Medusa head, the baroque prints, the body-conscious designs. Donatella Versace's brand is unapologetic desire. Aphrodite's Italian villa.

**Versace → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The Michael Kors acquisition.

**Dolce and Gabbana → [Aphrodite](/aphrodite/)** — The Sicilian aesthetic, the sensual campaigns, the Catholic iconography. Aphrodite's Sicily.

**Dolce and Gabbana → [Nemesis](/nemesis/)** — The racist advertising scandals, the boycott in China. Nemesis answered.

**Armani → [Aphrodite](/aphrodite/)** — Giorgio Armani invented power dressing for the 1980s. The deconstructed jacket, the neutral palette, the quiet luxury. Aphrodite's boardroom.

**Armani → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The $2 billion empire. Hotels, cosmetics, home.

**Burberry → [Artemis](/artemis/)** — The British heritage brand. The trench coat was invented for military officers in the trenches. Artemis governs the wilderness and the hunt; Burberry governs the weather.

**Burberry → [Nemesis](/nemesis/)** — The "chav" association, the burning of unsold stock, the declining relevance. Nemesis answers.

**Ralph Lauren → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The American dream in clothing form. The polo player, the Hamptons aesthetic, the WASP aspiration. Ralph Lauren is Ploutos's American wardrobe.

**Ralph Lauren → [Aphrodite](/aphrodite/)** — The romanticised Americana. The desire for a life you do not have.

**Tommy Hilfiger → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The preppy American brand. The red, white, and blue flag. Ploutos's preppy Americana.

**Tommy Hilfiger → [Nike](/nike/)** — The sailing sponsorship. The Hilfiger Cup.

**Calvin Klein → [Aphrodite](/aphrodite/)** — The minimalism, the provocative advertising (Brooke Shields, Mark Wahlberg, Kate Moss). Aphrodite's underwear.

**Calvin Klein → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The PVH-owned brand.

**Victoria's Secret → [Aphrodite](/aphrodite/)** — The lingerie brand that defined "sexy" for a generation. The Angels, the fashion show, the Fantasy Bra. Aphrodite's American lingerie.

**Victoria's Secret → [Nemesis](/nemesis/)** — The declining relevance, the Jeffrey Epstein connection, the exit of the Angels. Nemesis answered.

**Savage X Fenty → [Aphrodite](/aphrodite/)** — Rihanna's inclusive lingerie brand. Aphrodite for every body.

**Skims → [Aphrodite](/aphrodite/)** — Kim Kardashian's shapewear brand. The body as foundation. Aphrodite's shapewear.

**Skims → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The $4 billion valuation.

**Spanx → [Aphrodite](/aphrodite/)** — Sara Blakely's shapewear empire. Aphrodite's foundation garment.

**Spanx → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The $1 billion valuation.

**Alo Yoga → [Artemis](/artemis/)** — The Los Angeles yoga brand. Alo implies "air, land, ocean." Artemis's elements.

**Alo Yoga → [Aphrodite](/aphrodite/)** — The influencer culture. The aspirational aesthetic.

**Beyond Yoga → [Artemis](/artemis/)** — The Los Angeles yoga brand.

**Vuori → [Artemis](/artemis/)** — The California activewear brand. "The Rise. The Shine."

**Outdoor Voices → [Artemis](/artemis/)** — The "Doing Things" activewear brand.

**Outdoor Voices → [Nemesis](/nemesis/)** — The bankruptcy. Nemesis answered.

**Girlfriend Collective → [Gaia](/gaia/)** — The sustainable activewear brand. Recycled materials, ethical production. Gaia's activewear.

**Pangaia → [Gaia](/gaia/)** — The sustainable materials science brand. The name implies "all Earth." Gaia's clothing.

**Pangaia → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The materials innovation -- seaweed fibre, recycled down, flower down.

**Stella McCartney → [Gaia](/gaia/)** — The sustainable luxury brand. No leather, no fur, organic cotton. Gaia's luxury.

**Stella McCartney → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The LVMH partnership.

**Stone Island → [Ares](/ares/)** — The Italian outerwear brand. The compass badge. The dyeing technology. Ares's Italian military.

**Stone Island → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The Moncler acquisition.

**CP Company → [Ares](/ares/)** — The Italian outerwear brand. The goggle jacket. Ares's goggles.

**Moncler → [Artemis](/artemis/)** — The French-Italian down jacket brand. The mountain heritage. Artemis's alpine wear.

**Moncler → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The $15 billion valuation.

**Canada Goose → [Artemis](/artemis/)** — The Canadian parka brand. The Arctic heritage. Artemis's Canadian parka.

**Canada Goose → [Nemesis](/nemesis/)** — The animal rights criticism. Nemesis answered.

**Reformation → [Gaia](/gaia/)** — The sustainable fashion brand. Refertilising the Earth through fashion.

**Reformation → [Aphrodite](/aphrodite/)** — The sexy sustainable aesthetic.

**Reformation → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The $1.5 billion valuation.

**Everlane → [Ma'at](/maat/)** — The "Radical Transparency" brand. Ma'at governs truth and balance. Everlane publishes factory costs.

**Everlane → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The growth and struggles.

**Kotn → [Ma'at](/maat/)** — The ethical Egyptian cotton brand.

**Kotn → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The B Corp certification.

**tentree → [Gaia](/gaia/)** — The brand that plants ten trees per item. Gaia's reforestation wardrobe.

**tentree → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The public company.

**prAna → [Gaia](/gaia/)** — The sustainable yoga and outdoor brand.

**prAna → [Artemis](/artemis/)** — The outdoor positioning.

**prAna → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The Columbia Sportswear acquisition.

**United By Blue → [Gaia](/gaia/)** — The brand that removes one pound of trash per item sold.

**United By Blue → [Artemis](/artemis/)** — The outdoor positioning.

**Fjallraven → [Artemis](/artemis/)** — The Swedish outdoor brand. The Kanken backpack.

**Fjallraven → [Gaia](/gaia/)** — The sustainability focus.

**Helly Hansen → [Poseidon](/poseidon/)** — The Norwegian outdoor brand. The sailing and fishing heritage. Poseidon's Norwegian rain gear.

**Helly Hansen → [Artemis](/artemis/)** — The ski heritage.

**Icebreaker → [Artemis](/artemis/)** — The New Zealand merino brand.

**Icebreaker → [Gaia](/gaia/)** — The natural fibre focus.

**Icebreaker → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The VF Corp acquisition.

**Smartwool → [Artemis](/artemis/)** — The American merino brand.

**Smartwool → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The VF Corp ownership.

**Red Wing Shoes → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The American heritage boot brand. The Iron Ranger, the Moc Toe. Hephaistos's American boots.

**Red Wing Shoes → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The family-owned wealth.

**Wolverine → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The American heritage boot brand. The 1000 Mile.

**Wolverine → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The public company.

**Danner → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The American heritage boot brand.

**Danner → [Ares](/ares/)** — The military heritage.

**Allen Edmonds → [Hephaistos](/hephaistos/)** — The American dress shoe brand.

**Allen Edmonds → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The Caleres acquisition.

**Tiffany and Co. → [Aphrodite](/aphrodite/)** — The engagement ring, the blue box, the Breakfast at Tiffany's cultural icon. Aphrodite's jeweller.

**Tiffany and Co. → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The LVMH acquisition for $15.8 billion.

**Cartier → [Aphrodite](/aphrodite/)** — The French jeweller. The Love bracelet, the Tank watch, the Panthere. Aphrodite's Parisian jeweller.

**Cartier → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The Richemont-owned luxury brand.

**Van Cleef and Arpels → [Aphrodite](/aphrodite/)** — The French jeweller. The Alhambra collection. Aphrodite's garden.

**Van Cleef and Arpels → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The Richemont-owned brand.

**Bulgari → [Aphrodite](/aphrodite/)** — The Italian jeweller. The Serpenti collection. Aphrodite's serpent.

**Piaget → [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The Richemont-owned brand.
