# SECTOR 7: MEDIA, ENTERTAINMENT, AND BROADCASTING

**The Walt Disney Company -> [Apollo](/apollo/)** — The House of Mouse. The animation empire, the theme parks, the streaming wars. Apollo governs the arts, and Disney is the most successful artistic enterprise in history. From Steamboat Willie to The Lion King to Frozen — Apollo's American animation.

**Disney -> [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The $200 billion market cap (at peak). The merchandising, the parks, the cruises. Ploutos's happiest place on Earth.

**Disney -> [Hermes](/hermes/)** — The global distribution. Disney+ in 100+ countries. Hermes's streaming satchel.

**Disney -> [Nemesis](/nemesis/)** — The declining stock, the succession crisis, the political controversies. Nemesis answers.

**Warner Bros. Discovery -> [Apollo](/apollo/)** — The merged media giant. HBO, Warner Bros., DC, CNN, Discovery. Apollo's conglomerate.

**Warner Bros. Discovery -> [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The $40 billion company.

**Warner Bros. Discovery -> [Nemesis](/nemesis/)** — The debt, the layoffs, the declining stock. Nemesis answers.

**Paramount Global -> [Apollo](/apollo/)** — The media conglomerate. CBS, Paramount Pictures, MTV, Nickelodeon, Comedy Central. Apollo's Viacom.

**Paramount -> [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The public company.

**Paramount -> [Nemesis](/nemesis/)** — The declining relevance, the merger attempts. Nemesis answers.

**NBCUniversal -> [Apollo](/apollo/)** — The Comcast-owned media giant. NBC, Universal Pictures, Peacock, Universal Studios. Apollo's Comcast.

**NBCUniversal -> [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The Comcast ownership.

**Netflix -> [Apollo](/apollo/)** — The streaming pioneer. The algorithm, the originals, the global reach. Apollo's streaming temple.

**Netflix -> [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The $200 billion market cap (at peak).

**Netflix -> [Nemesis](/nemesis/)** — The password sharing crackdown, the ad tier, the stock volatility. Nemesis answers.

**Amazon Prime Video -> [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The streaming service bundled with Prime. The $15 billion content budget. Ploutos's streaming service.

**Amazon -> [Hermes](/hermes/)** — The global distribution.

**Apple TV+ -> [Apollo](/apollo/)** — The premium streaming service. The Morning Show, Ted Lasso, Severance. Apollo's Cupertino studio.

**Apple TV+ -> [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The Apple wealth.

**Hulu -> [Apollo](/apollo/)** — The streaming service. The FX partnership. Apollo's Disney subsidiary.

**Hulu -> [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The Disney ownership.

**HBO Max / Max -> [Apollo](/apollo/)** — The premium streaming service. The Sopranos, Game of Thrones, Succession. Apollo's premium tier.

**HBO -> [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The Warner Bros. Discovery ownership.

**Peacock -> [Apollo](/apollo/)** — The NBCUniversal streaming service.

**Peacock -> [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The Comcast ownership.

**Paramount+ -> [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The Paramount Global ownership.

**Discovery+ -> [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The Warner Bros. Discovery ownership.

**AMC+ -> [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The AMC Networks ownership.

**Starz -> [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The Lionsgate ownership.

**Showtime -> [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The Paramount Global ownership.

**Epix -> [Apollo](/apollo/)** — The premium cable channel.

**Epix -> [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The MGM ownership.

**MGM+ -> [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The Amazon ownership.

**YouTube -> [Ploutos](/ploutos/)** — The Google ownership.

**YouTube -> [Hermes](/hermes/)** — The global distribution.

# CONCLUSION: THE PUNICODEX FLAGSHIP TEMPLE ATLAS

This document represents a comprehensive mapping of the modern commercial world to the mythological archetypes of the Punicodex network. Spanning twenty-four major sectors and thousands of individual brand-to-temple connections, the Atlas demonstrates the many-to-many philosophy at the heart of the Punicodex vision: no single brand owns a deity, and no deity is limited to a single brand.

The connections made in this atlas are not arbitrary. Each mapping follows a consistent methodology:

1. **Primary Resonance**: The core business activity of the brand maps to a deity whose domain directly reflects that activity. Nike, the goddess of victory, claims athletic brands. Hermes, the messenger, claims logistics and communications. Demeter, the earth mother, claims agriculture and food production.

2. **Secondary Resonance**: The brand's business model, ownership structure, or financial profile maps to Ploutos (wealth) for public companies, or Nemesis for brands facing controversies, crises, or downfalls.

3. **Tertiary Resonance**: The brand's cultural positioning, design philosophy, or historical narrative maps to additional deities that capture subtler aspects of the brand identity.

This many-to-many approach creates a rich, multidimensional mapping where a single brand like Amazon can simultaneously claim Hermes (logistics), Ploutos (wealth), and Nemesis (labor controversies), while a single deity like Athena can simultaneously claim McKinsey (strategy), the Pulitzer Prize (excellence), and the Detection Club (mystery).

The atlas is intentionally exhaustive. It maps global supermajors and local cooperatives, publicly traded giants and family-owned estates, technology disruptors and heritage manufacturers. The goal is not to create a curated shortlist but to demonstrate the universal applicability of the Punicodex archetypal framework to the entire spectrum of human commerce.

As the Punicodex network grows, this atlas serves as both a reference document and an invitation. Brands listed here are not officially affiliated with the Punicodex - this is an audit trail of archetypal resonance, a demonstration of how the mythological map overlays the commercial world. The actual temple sponsorships, Herald features, and Reliquary partnerships will emerge through direct engagement between the Punicodex network and the brands themselves.

The work continues. New sectors, new brands, and new connections will be added as the commercial landscape evolves. The Punicodex is a living system, and this atlas is a living document.

*Compiled for the Punicodex audit trail. Thousands of connections made. The work is never done.*
